    window.$__t = function(key) {
      if (window.LANG[key] === undefined) {
        // console.log(key);
      }
      return window.LANG[key] || key;
    }

    window.__defaultLang = 'vn';
    window.__usdRate = '1';
    window.__atmDiscount = '0';

    window.$formatCurrency = function(number, currency = 'VND', maxinum = 2) {
      const lang = window.__defaultLang || 'vn';
      let rate = parseFloat(window.__usdRate);
      if (!Number.isFinite(rate) || rate <= 0) {
        rate = 1;
      }

      let amount = Number(number) || 0;

      if (lang !== 'vn') {
        amount = amount / rate;

        return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', {
          style: 'currency',
          currency: 'USD',
          minimumFractionDigits: 0,
          maximumFractionDigits: maxinum,
        }).format(amount);
      }

      return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN', {
        style: 'currency',
        currency: 'VND',
        minimumFractionDigits: 0,
        maximumFractionDigits: maxinum,
      }).format(amount);
    }

    window.__DEFAULT_THEME = 'light';
  </script>

<script>
  (function() {
    const SPEED = 80; // pixels per second — consistent for all marquees

    function setMarqueeDuration(el) {
      if (!el) return;
      var container = el.closest('.marquee-container');
      // Total travel = content width + container width (because of padding-left: 100%)
      var totalDistance = el.scrollWidth + (container ? container.offsetWidth : 0);
      el.style.animationDuration = (totalDistance / SPEED) + 's';
    }

    function escapeHtml(s) {
      return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function(c) {
        return ({
          '&': '&amp;',
          '<': '&lt;',
          '>': '&gt;',
          '"': '&quot;',
          "'": '&#39;'
        })[c];
      });
    }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
      var noticeEl = document.querySelector('[data-notice-content]');
      var purchasesEl = document.querySelector('[data-purchases-content]');

      // Initial pass for any non-deferred marquees
      document.querySelectorAll('.marquee-content').forEach(setMarqueeDuration);

      if (!noticeEl && !purchasesEl) return;

      axios.get('/api/home/banner-notices')
        .then(function(res) {
          var data = res && res.data ? res.data : {};
          if (noticeEl && data.notice) {
            var domain = escapeHtml(data.notice.domain || '');
            var text = escapeHtml(data.notice.text || '-');
            noticeEl.innerHTML =
              '<span class="inline-flex items-center rounded bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 font-bold px-1.5 py-0 mr-1.5">' +
              domain + '</span>' + text;
            setMarqueeDuration(noticeEl);
          }
          if (purchasesEl) {
            purchasesEl.innerHTML = data.recent_purchases_html || '';
            setMarqueeDuration(purchasesEl);
          }
        })
        .catch(function() {
          // Leave skeleton in place on failure — no fallback content needed
        });
    });
  })();
</script>

<button type="button" data-te-ripple-init="" data-te-ripple-color="light" class="!fixed bottom-5 right-5 hidden rounded-full bg-primary p-3 mb-[50px] text-xs font-medium uppercase leading-tight text-white shadow-md transition duration-150 ease-in-out hover:bg-red-700 hover:shadow-lg focus:bg-red-700 focus:shadow-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-0 active:bg-red-800 active:shadow-lg" id="btn-back-to-top">
  <svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" data-prefix="fas" class="h-4 w-4" role="img" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 448 512">
    <path fill="currentColor" d="M34.9 289.5l-22.2-22.2c-9.4-9.4-9.4-24.6 0-33.9L207 39c9.4-9.4 24.6-9.4 33.9 0l194.3 194.3c9.4 9.4 9.4 24.6 0 33.9L413 289.4c-9.5 9.5-25 9.3-34.3-.4L264 168.6V456c0 13.3-10.7 24-24 24h-32c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24V168.6L69.2 289.1c-9.3 9.8-24.8 10-34.3.4z">
    </path>
  </svg>
</button>
<script>
  // Get the button
  const mybutton = document.getElementById("btn-back-to-top");

  // When the user scrolls down 20px from the top of the document, show the button

  const scrollFunction = () => {
    if (
      document.body.scrollTop > 20 ||
      document.documentElement.scrollTop > 20
    ) {
      mybutton.classList.remove("hidden");
    } else {
      mybutton.classList.add("hidden");
    }
  };
  const backToTop = () => {
    window.scrollTo({
      top: 0,
      behavior: "smooth"
    });
  };

  // When the user clicks on the button, scroll to the top of the document
  mybutton.addEventListener("click", backToTop);

  window.addEventListener("scroll", scrollFunction);
</script>

<script>
  function toggleDepositModal() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('deposit-modal');
    if (modal.classList.contains('hidden')) {
      modal.classList.remove('hidden');
      setTimeout(() => {
        modal.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        modal.querySelector('div').classList.remove('scale-95');
        modal.querySelector('div').classList.add('scale-100');
      }, 10);
    } else {
      modal.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
      modal.querySelector('div').classList.add('scale-95');
      modal.querySelector('div').classList.remove('scale-100');
      setTimeout(() => {
        modal.classList.add('hidden');
      }, 300);
    }
  }
  // Close modal when clicking outside
  document.getElementById('deposit-modal').addEventListener('click', function(e) {
    if (e.target === this) {
      toggleDepositModal();
    }
  });
</script>

<script>
  (function() {
    var el = document.getElementById('online-counter-num');
    var POLL = 30000;

    function fetchCount() {
      var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
      xhr.open('GET', '/api/online-users-count');
      xhr.onload = function() {
        if (xhr.status === 200) {
          try {
            var data = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText);
            el.textContent = data.total || 0;
          } catch (e) {}
        }
      };
      xhr.send();
    }

    // Delay initial fetch to let page load first
    setTimeout(function() {
      fetchCount();
      setInterval(fetchCount, POLL);
    }, 3000);
  })();
</script>
  
    <script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
      const list = document.getElementById('banner-stats-list');
      if (!list) return;
      const mode = list.dataset.mode;
      const emptyText = "D\u1eef li\u1ec7u \u0111ang b\u1ecb \u1ea9n";
      const rankIcons = {
        1: 'noto:1st-place-medal',
        2: 'noto:2nd-place-medal',
        3: 'noto:3rd-place-medal'
      };
      const rankBg = {
        1: 'bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-500/20 to-yellow-500/5',
        2: 'bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-300/20 to-slate-300/5',
        3: 'bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-600/15 to-amber-600/5',
      };

      const escape = (s) => String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({
        '&': '&amp;',
        '<': '&lt;',
        '>': '&gt;',
        '"': '&quot;',
        "'": '&#39;'
      } [c]));

      const renderRow = (item, rank) => {
        const isTop3 = rank <= 3;
        const rowCls = isTop3 ? rankBg[rank] : 'hover:bg-slate-50/50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700/30';
        const rankCell = isTop3 ?
          `<iconify-icon icon="${rankIcons[rank]}" class="shrink-0 text-2xl"></iconify-icon>` :
          `<span class="shrink-0 w-7 h-7 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 group-hover:bg-primary/10 group-hover:text-primary transition-colors duration-200">${rank}</span>`;
        const amountCls = rank === 1 ? 'text-yellow-600 dark:text-yellow-400' : 'text-red-500 dark:text-red-400';
        const nameCls = isTop3 ? 'text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 group-hover:text-primary' : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 group-hover:text-primary';
        return `<li class="deposit-row group flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 cursor-default ${rowCls}">${rankCell}<span class="flex-1 text-sm font-semibold truncate transition-colors duration-200 ${nameCls}">${escape(item.username)}</span><span class="shrink-0 text-sm font-bold ${amountCls}">${escape(item.total_formatted)}</span></li>`;
      };

      axios.get('/api/home/banner-stats', {
          params: {
            mode
          }
        })
        .then(({
          data
        }) => {
          if (!data?.items?.length) {
            list.innerHTML = `<li class="flex items-center justify-center h-full py-8"><p class="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 text-sm">${escape(emptyText)}</p></li>`;
            return;
          }
          list.innerHTML = data.items.map((item, i) => renderRow(item, i + 1)).join('');
        })
        .catch(() => {
          list.innerHTML = `<li class="flex items-center justify-center h-full py-8"><p class="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 text-sm">${escape(emptyText)}</p></li>`;
        });
    });
  </script>
  <script>
    (function() {
      const root = document.getElementById('right-panel-tabs');
      if (!root) return;
      const STORAGE_KEY = 'rightPanelActiveTab';
      const TABS = ["card","top","bank"];
      const DEFAULT_TAB = "card";
      const btns = root.querySelectorAll('.right-panel-tab-btn');
      const panels = root.querySelectorAll('[data-tab-panel]');

      // Force Turnstile render once card panel is shown. CF auto-render can flake
      // when the widget element starts hidden (display:none) on page load.
      const ensureCardTurnstile = () => {
        const ts = root.querySelector('[data-tab-panel="card"] .cf-turnstile');
        if (!ts || !window.turnstile) return;
        if (ts.querySelector('iframe')) return; // already rendered
        try {
          window.turnstile.render(ts);
        } catch (e) {
          /* noop — likely double-render */
        }
      };

      const setActive = (tab, persist = true) => {
        btns.forEach((b) => b.classList.toggle('is-active', b.dataset.tab === tab));
        panels.forEach((p) => p.classList.toggle('hidden', p.dataset.tabPanel !== tab));
        if (tab === 'card') ensureCardTurnstile();
        if (persist) {
          try {
            localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, tab);
          } catch (e) {
            /* noop */
          }
        }
      };

      btns.forEach((b) => b.addEventListener('click', () => setActive(b.dataset.tab)));

      let saved = null;
      try {
        saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      } catch (e) {
        /* noop */
      }
      setActive(TABS.includes(saved) ? saved : DEFAULT_TAB, false);
    })();
  </script>
    <script src="https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/api.js" async="" defer=""></script>

    <script type="text/javascript" src="//cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/slick-carousel@1.8.1/slick/slick.min.js"></script>
    <script type="module">
      $('.slick-responsive').slick({
        slidesToShow: 6,
        slidesToScroll: 1,
        autoplay: true,
        autoplaySpeed: 3000,
        prevArrow: '',
        nextArrow: '',
        responsive: [{
          breakpoint: 768,
          settings: {
            slidesToShow: 3
          }
        }]
      });

      // Banner carousel with Swiper
      if (document.querySelector('.banner-swiper')) {
        const bannerSwiper = new Swiper('.banner-swiper', {
          slidesPerView: 1,
          spaceBetween: 0,
          loop: true,
          observer: true,
          observeParents: true,
          watchSlidesProgress: true,
          autoplay: {
            delay: 4000,
            disableOnInteraction: false,
            pauseOnMouseEnter: true,
          },
          pagination: {
            el: '.banner-swiper .swiper-pagination',
            clickable: true,
          },
          navigation: {
            nextEl: '.banner-swiper .swiper-button-next',
            prevEl: '.banner-swiper .swiper-button-prev',
          },
        });
        requestAnimationFrame(function() {
          bannerSwiper.updateSize();
        });
      }

      // ─── Group Search Filter ──────────────────────────────────────────
      (function() {
        function removeVietnameseTones(str) {
          return str.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
            .replace(/đ/g, 'd').replace(/Đ/g, 'D').toLowerCase();
        }

        let debounceTimers = {};

        document.querySelectorAll('.group-search-input').forEach(function(input) {
          const gridId = input.getAttribute('data-target-grid');
          const grid = document.getElementById(gridId);
          const emptyEl = document.getElementById('empty-' + gridId);
          const countEl = document.getElementById('count-' + gridId);
          const clearBtn = input.closest('.group-search-box').querySelector('.group-search-clear');

          if (!grid) return;

          function filterCards() {
            const query = removeVietnameseTones(input.value.trim());
            const cards = grid.querySelectorAll('.product-card-wrapper');
            let visibleCount = 0;

            cards.forEach(function(card) {
              const name = removeVietnameseTones(card.getAttribute('data-group-name') || '');
              if (!query || name.includes(query)) {
                card.classList.remove('group-hidden');
                visibleCount++;
              } else {
                card.classList.add('group-hidden');
              }
            });

            // Toggle empty state
            if (emptyEl) {
              emptyEl.classList.toggle('hidden', visibleCount > 0 || !query);
            }

            // Toggle count
            if (countEl) {
              if (query) {
                countEl.textContent = visibleCount + '/' + cards.length;
                countEl.classList.remove('hidden');
              } else {
                countEl.classList.add('hidden');
              }
            }

            // Toggle clear button
            if (clearBtn) {
              clearBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', !input.value);
            }
          }

          input.addEventListener('input', function() {
            clearTimeout(debounceTimers[gridId]);
            debounceTimers[gridId] = setTimeout(filterCards, 150);
          });

          if (clearBtn) {
            clearBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
              input.value = '';
              filterCards();
              input.focus();
            });
          }
        });
      })();
    </script>
